Then it claimed there was **no defect at all** and the work was curator-only — **also wrong, and that one nearly dismissed a live defect.** **⚠️ THE PREMISE IS TRUE OF THE CODE AND FALSE OF THE CORPUS, WHICH IS THE GENERAL LESSON:** `normalizeBareMath` returns immediately on **any** delimiter (`frontend/lib/math-normalization.ts:264-266`), and **313 of 339** backslash-bearing questions plus **470 of 498** explanations already contain one, so the repair fires on almost nothing that actually exists. Verifying that a repair is *wired up* says nothing about whether it *reaches the data*. **THE ACTUAL MECHANISM IS DELIMITER MIS-PAIRING, NOT MISSING DELIMITERS.** A question reading *"if an asset costs `$50,000` … `$A = P \times \frac{i(1+i)^n}{(1+i)^n-1}$`"* has its CURRENCY `$` open a math span that closes on the formula's `$`; KaTeX fails on the enclosed text and `renderMathSegment` re-emits the source — **zero rendered math, raw LaTeX on screen.** The mirror case swallows prose into italics instead. **Population: 22 questions + 49 explanations carrying `$<digit>` with two or more `$`.** **⚠️ A FIX AIMED AT `normalizeBareMath` WOULD BE A NO-OP HERE — do not scope one.** **Two adjacent gaps found by the same pass:** `app/shared/study-packs/[id]/page.tsx:69,75,81` render summary, keyConcepts and fullNotes as raw `{value}` with **no math rendering at all** (372 production summaries carry a backslash), and `SummaryMarkdown` never calls `normalizeBareMath` so bare math in a summary is never repaired (12 production summaries). Allowlist gaps proven by harness: `\to`, `\text{m/s}`, `90^\circ`. **⚠️⚠️ STILL OPEN. `v0.141.0` ATTEMPTED THE FIX AND REVERTED IT AT SIGNOFF — a cold falsification pass measured it as a NET REGRESSION, 385 production strings WORSE against 3 BETTER.** This row was briefly marked FIXED during that signoff and the marking is corrected here rather than quietly removed. **⚠️ WHY THE ATTEMPT FAILED, because it constrains every future one:** `startsCurrencyAmount` decided money-vs-maths from **the single character immediately after the digits**, and that character cannot discriminate — the dominant legitimate maths shape puts a space or an operator exactly where the predicate looked for a currency boundary. `$1.5 \times 10^4$` matched `1.5`, saw a space, was classified as money, its opener was skipped, **and a string that rendered CORRECTLY before the change printed raw after it.** Scientific notation, arithmetic steps (`$0.4 + 0.5 = 0.9$`) and ratios (`$4:5$`) all broke, concentrated in explanations and working solutions — the exact surface the release was named for. **⚠️ THE GUARD TESTED THE CLAUSE, NOT THE POPULATION, and that is the transferable lesson:** the release's own regression guard was `$3x^2$`, which survives ONLY because `x` is a letter. Nothing tested `$3 \times 4$`. **FIX CONSTRAINT for whoever takes this next: the character after the digits cannot decide it. Any replacement must decide on the WHOLE CANDIDATE SPAN — e.g. refuse the currency reading when the span up to its valid close contains a LaTeX command or a maths relation — and MUST be measured against the production corpus before shipping.** The corpus is 1,158 distinct strings, reproducible with a UNION over `study_packs.quiz`, `quiz_questions`, `generated_quizzes.questions` and `challenge_quiz_question_bank.question` filtered on a regex matching `$` + digits + an optional decimal, followed by either a character outside `[A-Za-z\=^_{$]` or end-of-string (the literal pattern is in the `v0.141.0` signoff commit message, kept out of this cell because its alternation character would split the table row). **⚠️ THE ORIGINAL DEFECT IS STILL REAL AND STILL UNFIXED** — a string carrying both money and a formula mis-pairs its delimiters — and `quiz-question-text.test.tsx` now carries it as a SKIPPED test plus three new guards (scientific notation, an arithmetic step, a ratio) that would have caught the reverted attempt. **⚠️ AND THE ROW'S OLD FRAMING IS STILL WRONG, SEPARATELY: it carried *"owner/curator work, hand-regenerate ~23 packs"* from `v0.78.0` onward. That was never going to fix anything — the defect reaches readers through the DISPLAY path.** **What `v0.141.0` DID ship:** `quiz-working-solution.tsx:89` adds `startsCurrencyAmount`, giving the `$` OPENER the currency-awareness the closer already had, so a string carrying both money and a formula pairs its delimiters correctly instead of handing KaTeX the whole sentence. `summary-markdown.tsx:56` normalises before `remark-math` tokenises (that plugin handles DELIMITED maths only). `app/shared/study-packs/[id]/page.tsx:76,83,90` renders summary, keyConcepts and fullNotes at all, where it previously emitted raw `{value}`. **⚠️ THE ROW'S OWN FRAMING WAS THE OBSTACLE AND IT IS WORTH SAYING PLAINLY: this row carried *"owner/curator work, hand-regenerate ~23 packs"* from `v0.78.0` onward, and that was never going to fix anything — the defect reaches readers through the DISPLAY path, so regenerating a pack repaired nothing while the renderer kept mis-pairing.** `summary-markdown.tsx:56` and the shared-page render sites are unaffected by the revert and remain shipped. **⚠️ A FIX AIMED AT `normalizeBareMath` WOULD STILL BE A NO-OP and the earlier warning stands** — it returns early on any delimiter, and 339/339 backslash-bearing questions and 498/498 explanations already carry one. **⚠️ STILL OPEN, deliberately out of scope:** `app/study/study-pack-results.tsx:114` renders keyConcepts raw (a fourth surface, one line, excluded so the release did not outgrow the tier set at kickoff); `SummaryMarkdown`'s repair reaches only the 36 summaries with no delimiter anywhere; and the allowlist gaps (`\to`, `\text{m/s}`, `90^\circ`) are untouched. Superseded: ~~NOTHING HERE IS FIXED — `v0.140.0` ships only a mutation-verified regression guard.~~ **⚠️ ONE THING THE PASS COULD NOT MEASURE:** bare math *inside* an already-delimited string is reachable by harness but has **no confirmed production instance** — two SQL attempts to size it were corrupted by escaping and their results are not trustworthy. Superseded: **CARRIED FORWARD — scoped into `v0.78.0` but NOT done, and deliberately not counted as shipped (2026-08-15).** It is owner/curator work requiring production access; the release closed without it rather than quietly dropping it. Re-run the sizing query before regenerating anything. **Originally: SCOPED INTO `v0.78.0`, 2026-08-14 (owner-selected at kickoff) — still owner/curator work, not engineering.** **Open, and newly indexed at the `v0.76.0` kickoff, 2026-08-14 — it was a release artifact until the work outlived two signoffs.** `v0.74.0` shipped two defences: a `Math notation` rule in all 10 content-generating developer prompts (fixes new content) and `lib/math-normalization.ts`, which repairs stored content **at display time**. The admin backfill was deliberately **dropped** on the sizing — a Java normaliser beside the TypeScript one would be two implementations of one rule, the hazard that release's own audit caught elsewhere. **So nothing is broken on screen; this is about the stored text.** The query lists affected notes with a `verdict` column and a companion query showing the offending text. **Worth re-running before regenerating anything:** the renderer fix may already make some `NEEDS_FIX` rows read correctly, which would shrink or empty the list | **`[EFFORT]`** — owner/curator time, not engineering, and not urgent precisely because the display-time normaliser holds. **Do not re-propose the admin backfill without a materially larger affected population**; if it is ever revived, the non-negotiables are recorded in `RELEASES.md` under `v0.74.0` | 2026-08-14 |

but the Public Library filter reads **public** notes (945), which the 905 curator notes essentially are — **so the backfill is curator-scoped only, learner notes are left alone, and Step 0's blast radius drops from ~5,550 to ~905.** **⚠️ UNGATED 2026-08-16 (owner call) — Phase 2 no longer waits on `2026-09-13`.** It touches Public Library discovery, but since `v0.79.0` the course/program chips are the primary filter vocabulary and audience is secondary, so the confound is marginal and is **recorded rather than waited on** — the same call `v0.78.0` made. **Removal of the authoring field, the filter chips and the `?audience=` param may ship now. Cost: one test-scoped dependency, Docker at test time (already the documented dev loop), ~15–30s container start. **⚠️ The cheaper "PREPARE only when a PG is reachable" option was considered and rejected as false comfort** — with no CI it is honour-system either way, and it covers strictly less; enabling Flyway on real PostgreSQL is the half that closes `v0.83.1`'s class too | none — shipped | 2026-08-28 |

| **Learning Connections — ratified five-phase direction (audit + plan)** | Owner-ratified product/UX direction, 2026-08-27, after an audit found two of the owner's four proposals already shipped, one that would corrupt a pre-committed measurement, and one blocked on data comparability.
